Quick Reference – Where to Use?

Living Room – Stucco, Venetian, Wood-like textures.
Bedroom – Limewash, Ombre, Sponge, Upholstered look.
Dining Area – Travertine, Metallic, Geometric.
Office / Commercial – Concrete, Microcement, Rustic.
